                                                     Corporate Introduction
                                                           公司簡介

Established in 1993, Kingdee International Software Group Company Limited (“Kingdee International” or “Kingdee”) is listed on the Main Board of the Hong
Kong Stock Exchange (stock code: 268.HK) and headquartered in Shenzhen, China, and has become a leader in the international market as a management
cloud SaaS vendor. Adhering to the core values of “Acting in all Conscience, with Integrity and Righteousness”, the Company takes the mission of “Empower
every enterprise to achieve phenomenal success.” Kingdee is committed to becoming the “most trustworthy enterprise service platform”.

As a global leader in enterprise management cloud SaaS solutions, Kingdee has demonstrated exceptional performance across cloud services, securing #1
positions in China's SaaS EA (Enterprise Application Cloud), SaaS ERM (Enterprise Resource Management Cloud), Financial Cloud, EPM, and PLM markets
according to IDC reports. The company has maintained its leadership for 20 consecutive years in China's growing enterprise application software sector,
stands as the sole Chinese vendor recognized in the Asia-Pacific SaaS ERP Mid-Small Market Competitiveness Report, and has achieved four consecutive
years of dominance in China's low-code/no-code aPaaS market. Notably, Kingdee's inclusion as the only Chinese management software provider in Gartner's
Market Guide for AI Code Assistants further validates its technological prowess in AI-driven solutions.

Kingdee’s major cloud service products have been selected by industry lighthouse customers, including Kingdee Cloud Cosmic (new generation enterprise-
level AI platform), Kingdee Cloud Constellation (large enterprise SaaS management solution), Kingdee Cloud Galaxy (high-growth enterprise SaaS
management solution), Kingdee Cloud Stellar (small and micro enterprise SaaS management solution). Kingdee has provided enterprise management cloud
products and services for more than 7.4 million enterprises, governments and organizations across the world.

金蝶國際軟件集團有限公司（「金蝶國際」或「金蝶」 ）始創於1993年，是香港聯交所主板上市公司（股票代碼：268.HK），總部
位於中國深圳，是全球領先的企業管理雲SaaS公司。以「致良知、走正道、行王道」為核心價值觀，以「予力企業，成就不凡」
為使命，致力成為「最值得託付的企業服務平台」。

作為全球領先的企業管理雲SaaS公司，金蝶在雲服務領域成果斐然。IDC報告顯示，金蝶在SaaS EA
                                                 （企業應用雲）、SaaS ERM
                                                                （企
業資源管理雲）、財務雲、EPM、PLM中國市場均排名第一；連續20年在中國成長型企業應用軟件市場佔有率居首，是亞太
SaaS ERP中小市場競爭力報告中唯一的中國廠商；連續4次獲得低代碼與零代碼aPaaS軟件中國市場佔有率第一。金蝶成功
入選Gartner《人工智能代碼助手市場指南》，是唯一入選的中國管理軟件廠商。

金蝶旗下的多款雲服務產品獲得標杆企業的青睞，包括金蝶雲 • 蒼穹（新一代企業級AI平台）、金蝶雲 • 星瀚（大型企業
SaaS管理雲）、金蝶雲 • 星空（高成長型企業SaaS管理雲）、金蝶雲 • 星辰（小微企業SaaS管理雲）等，已為世界範圍內超過
740萬家企業、政府与組織提供企業管理雲產品及服務。

Lighting the Path: AI-First, Subscription First, Accelerating                                  我們與光同行，堅持AI優先、訂閱優先，
Globalization                                                                                  發力國際化

In 2024, Kingdee Cloud revenue contribution reached 81.6% and cloud subscription               2024年，金 蝶 雲 服 務 收 入 佔 比 攀 升 至81.6%，訂 閱

42.9% to RMB934 million, validating the effectiveness of our Subscription-First strategy.      42.9%至9.34億 元，金 蝶 雲 • 蒼 穹 和 星 瀚、星 空、星
Product resilience was demonstrated through Net Dollar Retention (NDR) rates of 108%           辰的續費率分別為108%、95%、93%，印證「訂閱優
for Kingdee Cloud Cosmic & Constellation, 95% for Kingdee Cloud Galaxy, and 93% for            先」戰略成效。全球化佈局加速，業務覆蓋新加坡、
Kingdee Cloud Stellar. Our global footprint expanded significantly, establishing operational   越南、泰國、印度尼西亞、馬來西亞及中東地區，
hubs across Singapore, Vietnam, Thailand, Indonesia, Malaysia, and the Middle East,            為中國企業架起出海快車道。

   The Group performed expected credit loss assessment of loans to third parties              本集團通過對具有類似信用風險特徵的貸
   collectively by grouping loans with similar credit risk characteristics. During the        款進行分組，共同對第三方貸款進行了預期

   debt instruments measured at amortized cost, contractual cash flows of debt                 本計量的債務工具合同現金流量、以公允價
   instruments measured at fair value through profit or loss, deposits with banks and          值計量且其變動計入損益的債務工具合同現
   other financial institutions, as well as customer credit risk, including outstanding        金流量、銀行和其他金融機構存款以及未收
   receivables.                                                                                應收款在內的客戶信用風險。

   short-term bank deposits, long-term bank deposits, and asset management                     短期銀行存款、長期銀行存款及資產管理產
   products (classified as financial assets measured at fair value through profit              品（歸類為以公允價值計量且其變動計入損益
   or loss), the Group’s management engages only with state-owned or reputable                 的金融資產）的信用風險，本集團管理層僅與
   financial institutions in mainland China and Hong Kong.                                     中國國有或信譽良好的中國大陸及香港的財
                                                                                               務機構進行交易。

   end customers.                                                                              售給最終客戶。

   within the approved credit limits.                                                          保經銷商在信用額度內採購。

     The Group’s remuneration policy is determined based on each employee’s                      本集團的薪酬政策主要是根據每位員工的崗
     role responsibilities, performance, exceptional contributions, years of service,            位 責 任、工 作 績 效、突 出 貢 獻 及 服 務 年 期，
     and prevailing market conditions. When assessing compensation for directors                 以及現行市場情況釐定；以及於評估應付予

2015 Share Award Scheme                                                                               二零一五年股份獎勵計劃

The 2015 Share Award Scheme was adopted by the Company on 4 December 2015 to                          本公司二零一五年十二月四日採納之二零一五年股
provide incentives and reward the contributions of key employees and Directors of the                 份獎勵計劃以激勵及獎勵本集團若干僱員及董事所
Group. The 2015 Share Award Scheme shall be valid and effective for a period of 10 years              作的貢獻。二零一五年股份獎勵計劃自採納之日起
commencing from the adoption date and it has been terminated on 21 January 2025.                      有效期為十年，已於二零二五年一月二十一日終止。

Equity-linked agreement                                                                                  股權相關協議

2023 subscription of new Shares                                                                          二零二三年認購新股份

entered into the subscription agreement (the “Subscription Agreement”), pursuant to which                LLC（「認購方」）訂立認購協議（「認購協議」     ），據此，
the Subscriber subscribed for 154,627,000 new Shares (the “Subscription Shares”) at the                  根據於二零二三年五月十八日舉行的股東週年大
price of HK$10.10 per Subscription Share (the “Subscription Price”) under the general                    會授予董事會的一般授權，認購方按每股配售股份
mandate granted to the Board at the AGM held on 18 May 2023 (the “Subscription”).                        10.10港元認購154,627,000股新股份（「認購股份」）
                                                                                                                                        （「認
                                                                                                         購」）。

preceding the date of signing of the Subscription Agreement. The aggregate nominal value                 股收市價為10.38港元。認購股份的票面價值總額為

Board Diversity Policy                                                                        董事會多元化政策

The Company has adopted a board diversity policy (the “Board Diversity Policy”) which         本公司已採納董事會成員多元化（「董事會多元化政
sets out the approach to achieve diversity in the Board which will include and make good      策」）政策。政策列載董事會為達致成員多元化而採
use of the differences in skills, backgrounds and industry experience, gender, knowledge      取的方針，其中將涵蓋且善用董事會各成員所具備
and other qualities of the members of the Board. These differences will be considered in      不同的技能、背景及行業經驗、性別、知識以及其
determining the optimum composition of the Board and all Board appointments will be           他特質。在組成最理想的董事會時，將考慮上述各
based on merits, having due regard to the overall effective functioning of the Board as a     方面的差異，而董事會所有委任均以用人唯才為原
whole. The Company believes that diversity can strengthen the performance of the Board,       則，且顧及到董事會整體的職能可有效發揮。本公
promote effective decision-making and better corporate governance and monitoring. The         司深信，成員多元化可提高董事會的績效、促進有

regard to the benefits of diversity of the Board members. Selection of candidates will be     標有否達成。董事會所有的委任均以有利於董事會

2.1   Risk Management Procedure                                                              2.1   風險管理流程

      The Company has designed its major risk management criteria, including risk                  本 公 司 設 計 了 風 險 識 別、風 險 評 估、風 險 應
      identification, risk assessment, risk response and risk control and reporting:               對、風險監控與報告主要風險管理環節：

      •      Risk identification: The Audit Committee reviews the effectiveness of the             •   風險識別：審核委員會每年檢討本集團
             Group’s internal control and risk management systems each year and will                   內部監控及風險管理系統成效，督促各
             urge all business units and functional departments to identify the internal               業務單元與職能部門識別營運過程中可
             and external risks that may exist during the course of operation.                         能存在的內外部風險。

      •      Risk assessment: All business units and functional departments must assess            •   風險評估：各業務單元與職能部門對識
             the possibility of the occurrence of identified risks and the extent of their             別的風險，從發生的可能性及影響程度
             impact and screen out major risks.                                                        進行評價，篩選出重大風險。

      •      Risk response: The risk responsibility department properly uses risk                  •   風險應對：風險責任部門對已識別的重
             avoidance, reduction, sharing or tolerance measures to draw up a risk                     大風險，結合本集團風險承受度，妥善
             response scheme according to identified major risks and the Group’s risk                  使用風險迴避、降低、分擔或承受等方
             tolerance, so that the Group can reasonably allocate resources for risk                   法制定風險應對方案，以促使本集團合
             response and reduce its overall risk level to an acceptable level.                        理調配資源應對風險，使本集團的整體
                                                                                                       風險水準降至可接受範圍。

      •      Risk control and reporting: The Group’s risks are controlled and reported             •   風險監控與報告：綜合利用風險預警指
             through the comprehensive use of risk pre-warning indicators, internal                    標、內部審計、定期風險總結報告等形
             audits and regular risk summary reports.                                                  式，對本集團風險進行監控與報告。

2.3   Response to Major Risks                                                                  2.3   重大風險應對

      During the Reporting Period, the Group audited and assessed the Company’s risk                 本集團在報告期根據企業風險管理框架，對
      according to the enterprise’s risk management framework and finalized measures                 本公司整體年度進行風險審核及評估，並制
      for major risk response and control to prevent or reduce the possibility of the                定 重 大 風 險 應 對、監 控 舉 措，防 止 或 緩 解 本
      occurrence of any major risk to the Company.                                                   公司重大風險發生的可能。

      The Company has steadily developed its operational business in the past year. Due              本公司在過去一年經營業務得到了穩健發展，
      to the fierce market competition of the industry, the Company’s market competition             由於所處行業市場競爭較為激烈，本公司在
      in relation to key customers and cloud services may be intensified and customers               重點客戶與雲業務方面的市場競爭可能加劇，
      may continue to change their preferences and have a higher expectation of cloud                客戶偏好不斷變化，對雲業務服務價值期望
      services. The marketing costs of the Company’s cloud services remain high and                  更高；本公司對雲業務市場推廣成本投入較
      the launch of a new business is faced with inherent market risks. At the same time, as         高，新 業 務 推 出 存 在 固 有 的 市 場 風 險；隨 著
      the Company’s brand influence grows, its intellectual property rights are infringed            本公司品牌影響力增強，本公司知識產權受
      more frequently and infringers conceal or destroy tort evidence so that it becomes             到 侵 害 行 為 增 多，侵 權 人 採 用 隱 匿、銷 毀 侵
      more difficult for the Company to safeguard its intellectual property rights. At the           權證據等方式，增加了本公司知識產權維權
      same time, with the promulgation of the Network Security Law, Data Security Law,               難 度。同 時 隨 著《網 絡 安 全 法》、《數 據 安 全
      Personal Information Protection Law and relevant administrative regulations, rules             法》、《個人信息保護法》及相關的行政法規、
      and norms, the state’s supervision of Internet platform enterprises in the PRC                 規章以及規範性文件的頒佈，國家對互聯網
      has become increasingly strict, and the Company also faces increasingly strict                 平台型企業的監管日已趨嚴，本公司也面臨
      compliance requirements and legal liabilities in terms of network security, data and           越 來 越 嚴 格 的 網 絡 安 全、數 據 與 隱 私 保 護、
      privacy protection, unfair competition, intellectual property rights and so on.                反不正當競爭、知識產權等方面的合規要求
                                                                                                     和法律責任。

      In order to respond to possible operational risks and guarantee the healthy and                本公司為了應對可能的經營風險，保障經營
      sustainable development of the operational business, the Company (i) has formed                業 務 健 康 持 續 發 展，(i)通 過 優 化 調 整 戰 略 組
      a cloud service development framework based on mutually synergetic development                 織，形成相互協同發展的雲業務發展框架，(ii)
      by optimizing and adjusting its strategic organization; (ii) has allocated a lot of            投入大量資源研發新技術與提升產品價值，
      resources to research and develop new technologies and enhance product value;                  (iii)鼓勵員工不斷產品創新、優化客戶體驗，
      (iii) has encouraged its employees to conduct constant product innovation and                  (iv)完 善 客 戶 服 務 生 態 鏈，(v)佈 局 並 致 力 於 非

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the consolidated financial     我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在由
statements as a whole are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error,      於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取得合理保證，
and to issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. We report our opinion solely       並出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。我們僅向閣下
to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards or       （作為整體）報告我們的意見，除此之外本報告別無
accept liability to any other person for the contents of this report. Reasonable assurance     其他目的。我們不會就本報告的內容向任何其他人
is a high level of assurance, but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance     士負上或承擔任何責任。合理保證是高水平的保證，
with ISAs will always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can         但不能保證按照《國際審計準則》進行的審計，在某
arise from fraud or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate,    一重大錯誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以由
they could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken on        欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或滙總起
the basis of these consolidated financial statements.                                          來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴綜合財務報表
                                                                                               所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重
                                                                                               大。

         report to the related disclosures in the consolidated financial statements or, if        性，則有必要在核數師報告中提請使用者注
         such disclosures are inadequate, to modify our opinion. Our conclusions are              意綜合財務報表中的相關披露。假若有關的
         based on the audit evidence obtained up to the date of our auditor’s report.             披露不足，則我們應當發表非無保留意見。
         However, future events or conditions may cause the Group to cease to continue as         我們的結論是基於核數師報告日止所取得

2.2   Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                                          2.2   綜合與權益會計原則

2.2.1 Subsidiaries                                                                               2.2.1 子公司

      Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over which the Group has           子公司為本集團所控制的實體（包括結構化實
      control. The Group controls an entity where the Group is exposed to, or has rights               體）。當本集團從或有權從與該實體的參與中
      to, variable returns from its involvement with the entity and has the ability to affect          獲得變動收益並有能力對這些收益產生影響
      those returns through its power to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries are         時，則 其 控 制 該 子 公 司。自 控 制 權 轉 移 至 本
      fully consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to the Group. They              集 團 之 日 起，子 公 司 被 合 併。其 自 喪 失 實 際
      are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                                            控制權之日起停止合併。

      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for business                             本集團的業務合併採用會計購買法核算（見附
      combinations by the Group (refer to Note 2.2).                                                   註2.2）。

      Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains on transactions                        集團內公司之間的交易、結餘及交易的未變
      between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated                    現 利 得 予 以 對 銷。未 變 現 損 失 亦 予 以 對 銷，
      unless the transaction provides evidence of an impairment of the transferred asset.              除非交易提供所轉撥資產的減值證據。子公
      Accounting policies of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                  司報告的數額已按需要作出改變，以確保與
      consistency with the policies adopted by the Group.                                              本集團採用的政策符合一致。

      Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries are shown                    子公司業績和股權中的非控股權益分別列示
      separately in the consolidated income statement, consolidated statement of                       於 綜 合 利 潤 表、綜 合 全 面 收 益 表、綜 合 權 益
      comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity and consolidated statement                  變動表和綜合財務狀況表。
      of financial position respectively.

2.2.2 Associates                                                                                 2.2.2 聯營

      Associates are all entities over which the Group has significant influence but not               聯營公司是集團對其有重大影響但不控制或
      control or joint control. This is generally the case where the Group holds between               聯 合 控 制 的 所 有 實 體。通 常 情 況 下，該 集 團
      20% and 50% of the voting rights. Investments in associates are accounted for                    擁 有20%至50%的 投 票 權。聯 營 公 司 的 投 資 在
      using the equity method of accounting (see Note 2.2.3 below), after initially being              最初按成本確認後，以權益會計方法核算（見
      recognised at cost.                                                                              附註2.2.3）。

2.2.3 Equity method                                                                              2.2.3 權益法

      Under the equity method of accounting, the investments are initially recognised at               權 益 法 下，投 資 賬 面 價 值 最 初 以 成 本 確 認，
      cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of the post-acquisition              之後卻按本集團持有投資對象得份額確認投
      profits or losses of the investee in profit or loss, and the Group’s share of                    資利潤或虧損以及其他綜合收益。從聯營公
      movements in other comprehensive income of the investee in other comprehensive                   司收到或應收的股息確認為投資賬面價值的
      income. Dividends received or receivable from associates are recognised as a                     減少。
      reduction in the carrying amount of the investment.

      Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-accounted investment equals                       若本集團在股本投資中所承擔的損失與本集
      or exceeds its interest in the entity, including any other unsecured long-term                   團在該實體（包括任何其他無擔保的長期應收
      receivables, the Group does not recognise further losses, unless it has incurred                 賬款）的權益相等或超過其權益，則本集團不
      obligations or made payments on behalf of the other entity.                                      承認進一步的損失，除非本集團已為該實體
                                                                                                       承擔了義務或代表該實體支付了款項。

2.2.4 Changes in ownership interests                                                          2.2.4 所有權權益的變化

      The Group treats transactions with non-controlling interests that do not result in            本集團將不會導致控制權喪失的非控股股權
      a loss of control as transactions with equity owners of the Group. A change in                交易視為與本集團股權所有者的交易。所有
      ownership interest results in an adjustment between the carrying amounts of the               者權益的變化導致控制權益和非控制權益的
      controlling and non-controlling interests to reflect their relative interests in the          賬面價值之間的調整，以反映它們在子公司
      subsidiary. Any difference between the amount of the adjustment to non-controlling            中的相對權益。對非控股權益的調整金額與
      interests and any consideration paid or received is recognised in a separate reserve          已付或已收的任何代價之間的任何差額，將
      within equity attributable to owners of the Company.                                          在歸屬於本集團股東的權益內的單獨儲備中
                                                                                                    確認。

      When the Group ceases to consolidate or equity account for an investment because              當本集團因控制權喪失、聯合控制或重大影
      of a loss of control or significant influence, any retained interest in the entity is         響而停止合併或將某項投資計入股本賬戶時，
      remeasured to its fair value with the change in carrying amount recognised in profit          本集團在該實體的任何保留權益將重新計量
      or loss. This fair value becomes the initial carrying amount for the purposes of              為其公允價值，計入損益的賬面金額將發生
      subsequently accounting for the retained interest as an associate or financial asset.         變 化。該 公 允 價 值 成 為 作 為 聯 營 企 業、合 資
      In addition, any amounts previously recognised in other comprehensive income                  企業或金融資產的保留權益的後續會計目的
      in respect of that entity are accounted for as if the Group had directly disposed of          的 初 始 賬 面 價 值。此 外，任 何 先 前 在 有 關 該
      the related assets or liabilities. This may mean that amounts previously recognised           實體的其他綜合收入中確認的金額，在會計
      in other comprehensive income are reclassified to profit or loss or transferred to            處理時均視為本集團已直接處置有關資產或
      another category of equity as specified permitted by applicable IFRS Accounting               負債。這可能意味著以前在其他綜合收入中
      Standards.                                                                                    確認的金額被重新分類為損益，或被轉移到

      The acquisition method of accounting is used to account for all business                          不論權益工具或其他資產是否被收購，會計
      combinations, regardless of whether equity instruments or other assets are                        的購買法都被用於所有的企業合併。為收購
      acquired. The consideration transferred for the acquisition of a subsidiary                       附屬公司而轉讓的代價包括：
      comprises the:

      •      fair values of the assets transferred                                                      •   轉讓資產的公允價值

      •      liabilities incurred to the former owners of the acquired business                         •   對被收購企業的前所有者承擔的責任

      •      equity interests issued by the Group                                                       •   集團發行的權益

      •      fair value of any asset or liability resulting from a contingent consideration             •   或有對價安排產生的任何資產或負債的
             arrangement, and                                                                               公允價值，以及

      •      fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in the subsidiary.                          •   子公司現有權益的公允價值

      Identifiable assets acquired and liabilities and contingent liabilities assumed in a              企業合併中取得的可辨認資產、負債和或有
      business combination are, with limited exceptions, measured initially at their fair               負債除個別情況外，最初按取得日的公允價
      values at the acquisition date. The Group recognises any non-controlling interest                 值計量。本集團按按公允價值或按非控股權
      in the acquired entity on an acquisition-by-acquisition basis either at fair value                益在被收購實體的可辨認淨資產中所佔的比
      or at the non-controlling interest’s proportionate share of the acquired entity’s net             例，按逐筆收購的方式確認其在被收購實體
      identifiable assets.                                                                              中的任何非控股權益。

      Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                                               購買相關成本在產生時支銷。

      The excess of the:                                                                                過量的：

      •      consideration transferred                                                                  •   支付的對價

      •      amount of any non-controlling interest in the acquired entity, and                         •   收購實體中任何非控制權益的金額，以
                                                                                                            及

      •      acquisition-date fair value of any previous equity interest in the acquired                •   被收購實體之前任何股權的收購日公允
             entity                                                                                         價值

      over the fair value of the net identifiable assets acquired is recorded as goodwill.              超過公允價值的淨可辨認資產收購記為商譽。
      If those amounts are less than the fair value of the net identifiable assets of the               如果該等金額低於被收購企業的可辨認資產
      business acquired, the difference is recognised directly in profit or loss as a                   淨值的公允價值，則差額直接確認為廉價收
      bargain purchase.                                                                                 購的損益。

2.6   Current and deferred income tax                                                             2.6   當期及遞延所得稅

      The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax payable on the current                 當期的所得稅費用或抵免額，是根據各司法
      period’s taxable income based on the applicable income tax rate for each                          管轄區的適用所得稅率，對當期應納稅所得
      jurisdiction adjusted by changes in deferred tax assets and liabilities attributable to           額計算的應納稅款，該稅率由因暫時性差異
      temporary differences and to unused tax losses.                                                   和未使用的稅損引起的遞延所得稅資產和負

(a)   Current income tax                                                                          (a)   當期所得稅

      where the Company and its subsidiaries operate and generate taxable income.                       日期已頒佈或實質上已頒佈的稅法計算的。
      Management periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect                     管理層會就適用稅法受到解釋的情況定期評
      to situations in which applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation and                 估納稅申報表中的頭寸，並考慮稅務機關是
      considers whether it is probable that a taxation authority will accept an uncertain               否有可能接受不確定的稅收待遇。本集團根
      tax treatment. The Group measures its tax balances either based on the most likely                據最可能的金額或預期價值計量其稅收餘額，
      amount or the expected value, depending on which method provides a better                         具體取決於哪種方法可以更好地預測不確定
      prediction of the resolution of the uncertainty.                                                  性的解決方法。

(b)   Deferred income tax                                                                         (b)   遞延所得稅

      Deferred income tax is recognised, using the liability method, on temporary                       遞延所得稅採用負債法，對資產和負債的計
      differences arising between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and their carrying            稅基礎與其在綜合財務報表中的賬面價值之
      amounts in the consolidated financial statements. However, deferred tax liabilities               間 產 生 的 暫 時 性 差 異 予 以 確 認。但 是，如 果
      are not recognised if they arise from the initial recognition of goodwill, the deferred           遞延稅項負債是由於初始確認商譽而產生的，
      income tax is not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition of an asset or              則不予確認；如果遞延所得稅是由於在企業
      liability in a transaction other than a business combination that at the time of the              合併以外的交易中對資產或負債的初始確認
      transaction affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss and does not give               而 產 生 的，則 不 計 入 遞 延 所 得 稅。該 交 易 既
      rise to equal taxable and deductible temporary differences. Deferred income tax                   不影響會計也不影響應稅損益，且不會產生
      is determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been enacted or substantially                  相等的應納稅和可抵扣的暫時性差異。遞延

       losses which uses a lifetime expected loss allowance for all trade receivables and             法計量預期信用損失，即對所有應收賬款及

       been grouped based on shared credit risk characteristics and the invoice days. The             用風險特徵和賬齡對應收賬款和合同資產進

       of contracts. The Group has therefore concluded that the expected loss rates for               上相同的風險特徵。因此，本集團得出結論，
       trade receivables within aging bucket are a reasonable approximation of the loss               賬齡時段內應收賬款的預期損失率是合同資
       rates for the contract assets.                                                                 產損失率的合理近似值。

       the invoice days of trade receivables across historical years and the corresponding            史年度的貿易應收款項和合同資產的發票天
       historical credit losses experienced within the period. The historical loss rates are          數以及該期間的相應歷史信用損失而估計的。
       adjusted to reflect current and forward-looking information on macroeconomic                   調整歷史損失率，以反映有關影響客戶清算
       factors affecting the ability of the customers to settle the receivables. The Group            應收款能力的宏觀經濟因素的當前和前瞻性
       has identified production price index, broad measure of money supply (M2), and                 資 訊。本 集 團 已 將 生 產 價 格 指 數、廣 義 貨 幣
       fixed asset investment price index as the main influencing factors, and accordingly            供應量、固定資產投資價格指數識別為主要
       adjusts the historical loss rates based on expected changes in these factors. The              影響因素，並根據其預期變化對歷史損失率
       Group would also monitor the counterparties individually and identify specific                 進 行 調 整。本 集 團 亦 會 監 察 交 易 方，並 識 別

3.3   Capital risk management                                                                 3.3   資金風險管理

      The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the Group’s ability             本集團的資金管理政策，是保障集團能持續
      to continue as a going concern in order to provide returns for owners and benefits            運營，以為股東提供回報和為其他權利持有
      for other stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce the             人提供利益，同時維持最佳的資本結構以降
      cost of capital.                                                                              低資金成本。

      In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust the                為了維持或調整資本結構，本集團可能會調
      amount of dividends paid to owners, return capital to shareholders, issue new                 整支付予股東的股利數額、向股東退還資本、
      shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                                         發行新股或出售資產以減低債務。

      Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors capital on the basis               與業內其他公司一樣，本集團利用資本負債
      of the gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as net debt divided by total capital.          比率監察其資本。此比率按照債務淨額除以
      Net debt is calculated as total borrowings (including “current and non-current                總資本計算。債務淨額為總借款（包括綜合財
      borrowings” and “current and non-current lease liabilities” as shown in the                   務狀況表所列的「流動及非流動借款」和「流
      consolidated statement of financial position) less cash and cash equivalents. Total           動及非流動租賃負債」）減去現金和現金等價
      capital is calculated as “equity” as shown in the consolidated statement of financial         物。總資本為「權益」
                                                                                                             （如綜合財務狀況表所列）
      position plus net debt.                                                                       加債務淨額。

      The gearing ratios at 31 December 2024 and 2023 were as follows:                              在二零二四年及二零二三年十二月三十一日，

(c)   The Group’s leasing activities and how these are accounted for                        (c)   本集團的租賃活動及其會計處理

      Company’s shares) for the same total proceeds is added to the denominator as the               上方式計算的股份數目，與假設購股權行使

                                                                                                     將加進分母，作為無需代價而發行之普通股。

40    Summary of other accounting policies                                                   40    其他會計政策匯總

40.1 Segment reporting                                                                       40.1 分部報告

      Operating segments are reported in a manner consistent with the internal reporting           經營分部按照向首席經營決策者提供的內部
      provided to the chief operating decision-maker. The chief operating decision-                報告貫徹一致的方式報告。首席經營決策者
      maker, who is responsible for allocating resources and assessing performance                 被認定為作出策略性決定的指導委員會負責
      of the operating segments, has been identified as the executive directors of the             分配資源和評估經營分部的表現。
      Company that makes strategic decisions.

40.2 Foreign currency translation                                                            40.2 外幣折算

(a)   Functional and presentation currency                                                   (a)   功能和列報貨幣

      Items included in the financial statements of each of the Group’s entities are               本集團每個主體的財務報表所列項目均以本
      measured using the currency of the primary economic environment in which the                 集團經營所在的主要經濟環境的貨幣計量（「功
      entity operates (the “functional currency”). The consolidated financial statements           能 貨 幣」）。綜 合 財 務 報 表 以 人 民 幣 列 報，人
      are presented in RMB, which is the Company’s functional and presentation                     民幣為本公司的功能貨幣及本集團的列報貨幣。
      currency.

(b)   Transactions and balances                                                              (b)   交易及結餘

      Foreign currency transactions are translated into the functional currency using              外幣交易採用交易或項目重新計量的估值日
      the exchange rates at the dates of the transactions. Foreign exchange gains and              期的匯率換算為功能貨幣。結算此等交易產
      losses resulting from the settlement of such transactions and from the translation           生的匯兌利得和損失以及將外幣計值的貨幣
      of monetary assets and liabilities denominated in foreign currencies at year end             資產和負債以年終匯率折算產生的匯兌利得
      exchange rates are generally recognised in profit or loss.                                   和損失在綜合利潤表確認。

      Foreign exchange gains and losses that relate to borrowings are presented in                 與借款有關的匯兌利得和損失在綜合利潤表
      the income statement, within finance costs. All other foreign exchange gains and             內的「財務收益或費用」中列報。所有其他匯
      losses are presented in the income statement on a net basis within “Other gains/             兌利得和損失以淨額在綜合利潤表內的「其他
      losses – net”.                                                                               利得╱虧損－淨額」中列報。

      Non-monetary items that are measured at fair value in a foreign currency are                 非貨幣性金融資產及負債（例如以公允價值計
      translated using the exchange rates at the date when the fair value was determined.          量且其變動計入損益的權益）的折算差額列報
      Translation differences on assets and liabilities carried at fair value are reported         為公允價值利得和損失的一部分。非貨幣性
      as part of the fair value gain or loss. For example, translation differences on non-         金融資產及負債（例如分類為可供出售的權益）
      monetary assets and liabilities such as equities held at fair value through profit           的折算差額包括在其他綜合收益中。
      or loss are recognised in profit or loss as part of the fair value gain or loss and
      translation differences on non-monetary assets such as equities classified as fair
      value through other comprehensive income are recognised in other comprehensive
      income.

40    Summary of other accounting policies (Continued)                                        40    其他會計政策匯總（續）

40.2 Foreign currency translation (Continued)                                                 40.2 外幣折算（續）

(c)   Group companies                                                                         (c)   集團公司

      The results and financial position of foreign operations (none of which has the               其功能貨幣與本集團的列報貨幣不同的所有
      currency of a hyper-inflationary economy) that have a functional currency different           集團內的主體（當中沒有惡性通貨膨脹經濟的
      from the presentation currency are translated into the presentation currency as               貨幣）的業績和財務狀況按如下方法換算為列
      follows:                                                                                      報貨幣：

      (i)     assets and liabilities for each statement of financial position presented             (i)     每份列報的財務狀況表內的資產和負債
              are translated at the closing rate at the date of that statement of financial                 按該財務狀況表日期的收市匯率換算；
              position;

      (ii)    income and expenses for each income statement are translated at average               (ii)    每份損益表內的收益和費用按平均匯率
              exchange rates (unless this average is not a reasonable approximation of                      換算（除非此匯率並不代表交易日期匯
              the cumulative effect of the rates prevailing on the transaction dates, in                    率的累計影響的合理約數；在此情況下，
              which case income and expenses are translated at the rate on the dates of                     收支項目按交易日期的匯率換算）；及
              the transactions); and

      (iii)   all resulting exchange differences are recognised in other comprehensive              (iii)   所有由此產生的匯兌差額在其他綜合收
              income.                                                                                       益中確認。

     Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction costs incurred.            一般及特定借款按公允價值並扣除產生的交
     Borrowings are subsequently stated at amortised cost; any differences between                    易費用為初始確認金額。借款其後按攤銷成

     method.                                                                                          損益表確認。

     costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some or all of the facility will        資將會很可能提取，該費用確認為貸款的交
     be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred until the draw-down occurs. To                  易 費 用。在 此 情 況 下，費 用 遞 延 至 貸 款 提 取
     the extent there is no evidence that it is probable that some or all of the facility will        為止。如沒有證據證明部分或全部融資將會
     be drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a pre-payment for liquidity services and                很可能被提取，則該費用資本化作為流動資
     amortised over the period of the facility to which it relates.                                   金服務的預付款，並按有關的融資期間攤銷。

     The difference between the carrying amount of a financial liability that has been                另一方的金融負債的賬面值與已付對價的差

      of the Group granted under the share-based compensation plans as appropriate.                 估集團授予的其他權益工具。

40.14 Provisions                                                                                40.14 準備

      Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present legal or constructive                    當本集團因已發生的事件而產生現有的法律
      obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that an outflow of resources will         或推定義務；很可能需要有資源的流出以結
      be required to settle the obligation; and the amount has been reliably estimated.               算 義 務；及 金 額 已 被 可 靠 估 計 時，當 作 出 準
      Provisions are not recognised for future operating losses.                                      備。準備不會就未來經營虧損確認準備。

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an outflow will            如有多項類似義務，其需要在結算中有資源
      be required in settlement is determined by considering the class of obligations as a            流出的可能性，則可根據義務的類別整體考
      whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to           慮。即使在同一義務類別所包含的任何一個
      any one item included in the same class of obligations may be small.                            項目相關的資源流出的可能性極低，仍須確
                                                                                                      認準備。

      Provisions are measured at the present value of management’s best estimate of                   撥備按管理層於報告期末償還現時義務所需
      the expenditure required to settle the present obligation at the end of the reporting           開支的最佳估計的現值計量。用於確定現值
      period. The discount rate used to determine the present value is a pre-tax rate                 的折現率是稅前利率，其反映了貨幣時間價
      that reflects current market assessments of the time value of money and the risks               值和負債特定風險的當前市場評估。隨著時
      specific to the liability. The increase in the provision due to the passage of time is          間的流逝，準備金的增加被確認為利息支出。
      recognised as interest expense.

40.15 Earnings per share                                                                        40.15 每股收益

(a)   Basic earnings per share                                                                  (a)   基本每股收益

      Basic earnings per share is calculated by dividing the profit attributable to owners            基本每股收益的計算方法為：公司所有者應
      of the Company, excluding any costs of servicing equity other than ordinary                     佔利潤，不包括除普通股以外的任何股本服
      shares by the weighted average number of ordinary shares outstanding during the                 務成本，按本財政年度已發行普通股的加權
      financial year, adjusted for bonus elements in ordinary shares issued during the                平均數量計算，並根據當年發行的普通股中
      year and excluding treasury shares.                                                             的獎金因素進行調整，不包括庫存股。

(b)   Diluted earnings per share                                                                (b)   稀釋每股收益

      Diluted earnings per share adjusts the figures used in the determination of basic               稀釋每股收益調整用於確定每股基本收益的
      earnings per share to take into account:                                                        數位，以顧及：

       •        the after income tax effect of interest and other financing costs associated          •    利息及其他融資成本的稅後效應與稀
                with dilutive potential ordinary shares, and                                               釋潛在普通股相關；

       •        the weighted average number of additional ordinary shares that would                  •    加權平均額外發行普通股的數量，假
                have been outstanding assuming the conversion of all dilutive potential                    設所有稀釋性潛在普通股的轉換。
                ordinary shares.

40.17 Leases                                                                                 40.17 租賃

     Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a corresponding liability at the           租賃在本集團可供租賃的資產使用當日確認
     date at which the leased asset is available for use by the Group.                            為使用權資產和相應的負債。


     Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease components. The Group allocates               合同可能同時包含租賃和非租賃組成部分。
     the consideration in the contract to the lease and non-lease components based on             本集團根據相對獨立價格將合同中的對價分
     their relative stand-alone prices. However, for leases of real estate for which the          配 給 租 賃 和 非 租 賃 部 分。然 而，對 於 本 集 團
     Group is a lessee, it has elected not to separate lease and non-lease components             為承租人的房地產租賃，它選擇不將租賃和
     and instead accounts for these as a single lease component.                                  非租賃組成部分分開，而是將其作為一個單
                                                                                                  獨的租賃組成部分進行會計處理。


     Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and contain a wide range of                租賃條款是根據個人情況協商確定的，其中
     different terms and conditions. The lease agreements do not impose any covenants             包含各種不同的條款和條件。除了出租人持
     other than the security interests in the leased assets that are held by the lessor.          有的租賃資產中的擔保權益外，租賃協議不
     Leased assets may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.                            施加任何其他約定。租賃資產不得用作借貸
                                                                                                  擔保。

      the related assets.

40.20 Impairment of non-financial assets                                                      40.20 非金融資產投資的減值

      Goodwill and intangible assets that have an indefinite useful life are not subject to        使用壽命不確定的商譽和無形資產不予攤銷，
      amortisation and are tested annually for impairment, or more frequently if events            並且每年進行減值測試，如果事件或情況變
      or changes in circumstances indicate that they might be impaired. Other assets are           化表明它們可能已減值，則更頻繁地進行測
      tested for impairment whenever events or changes in circumstances indicate that              試。當事件或情況變化表明賬面價值可能無
      the carrying amount may not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised for             法 收 回 時，會 對 其 他 資 產 進 行 減 值 測 試。對
      the amount by which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable amount.              於資產的賬面價值超過其可收回金額的金額
      The recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs of disposal         確認減值損失。可收回金額是資產的公允價
      and value in use. For the purposes of assessing impairment, assets are grouped               值減去處置費用和使用價值中的較高者。為
      at the lowest levels for which there are separately identifiable cash inflows which          了評估減值，將資產分類為具有可單獨識別
      are largely independent of the cash inflows from other assets or groups of assets            的現金流入的最低級別，這些現金流入在很
      (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets other than goodwill that suffered an           大程度上獨立於來自其他資產或資產組（現金
      impairment are reviewed for possible reversal of the impairment at the end of each           產生單位）的現金流入。除商譽外，已減值的
